HEADLINE: Firm expands premium real estate portfolio with Chiroma’s court development    Byline  BAM Projects and Properties Ltd has announced the introduction of Chiroma’s Court, a premium residential development located in Gwarinpa, Abuja, as part of its ongoing commitment to delivering quality real estate investment opportunities across the Federal Capital Territory.  The development, which comprises elegantly designed four-bedroom terrace duplexes with Boys’ Quarters (BQ), further strengthens the company’s growing portfolio of residential and investment-focused projects within Abuja’s most sought-after locations.  Speaking on the project, the Managing Director of BAM Projects and Properties Ltd said the development reflects the company’s vision of creating communities that deliver both lifestyle value and long-term investment returns.  “At BAM, our focus goes beyond constructing buildings. We are committed to developing properties in strategic locations that provide comfort, security, and strong appreciation potential for homeowners and investors alike,” he said.  According to the company, Chiroma’s Court was strategically conceived to meet the growing demand for premium housing within Gwarinpa, one of Abuja’s most established residential districts.  The project joins a portfolio of notable developments by BAM Projects and Properties, including Belham Estate in Karsana, Expressive View along Airport Road, and Project 77 in Asokoro, all of which have been designed to address the evolving needs of homeowners and investors seeking quality developments in high-growth locations.  The company noted that increasing urbanisation, infrastructure development, and rising demand for quality housing continue to create opportunities for real estate investment across Abuja.  “Our objective is to provide investors with access to developments that combine quality construction, strategic positioning, and long-term value creation. We believe real estate remains one of the most reliable pathways to wealth preservation and sustainable financial growth,” the Managing Director added.  Industry observers continue to identify Abuja as one of Nigeria’s most attractive real estate markets, driven by population growth, infrastructure expansion, and increasing demand for residential and commercial developments.  With the introduction of Chiroma’s Court and its expanding portfolio of projects, BAM Projects and Properties says it remains focused on contributing to Abuja’s urban growth while creating investment opportunities that deliver lasting value.  Interested investors and prospective homeowners can obtain further information on BAM Projects and Properties social media platforms.
